# 承德露露股份公司 深度研究与条件估值

本报告基于公开信息整理，用于帮助理解该公司及其所处行业，**不构成任何投资建议**，也不提供买入、卖出或目标价判断。报告中的分析框架与事实判断仅供研究参考，投资者应自行核实并独立决策，据此操作风险自担。报告不保证所引信息的完整性与时效性；标注“待核实”的内容尤需进一步验证。

# 速览

研究信息尚有缺口；以下结论以固定共享快照及注明的经营条件为限。

财务信息质量：UNKNOWN；股价节奏坐标：缺同口径长期价格与盈利时序。

最大证伪点：逐项经营假设的未来失效条件仍待验证。

The issuer discloses plant beverage manufacturing and separately reports cash, debt and cash-flow information.

以上经营结论绑定与每日研究相同的共享核心；完整引用版本见附录K。

## 9. 估值口径、条件估值与现价要求

### 9.1 方法适用性

固定审核方法适用性为 CONDITIONAL；研究条件与限制：
|限制|
|---|
|Consolidated PRC-GAAP CNY yuan/SHARE; do not substitute parent-company values.|
|2025FY basis with actual 2026 acquisition is not historical PIT or a current balance.|
|2025 CFO includes276916417.60 cash WC release; do not extrapolate to perpetual FCFF.|
|Debt/lease and restricted-share repurchase obligations need consistent bridge and share policy.|
|AP includes capital-project payables; operating WC definitions and growth/reinvestment must be explicit.|
|D&amp;A, lease renewals, capitalized leases, maintenance-vs-expansion require a consistent policy; project capex is not maintenance.|
|Related finance-company deposits are unrestricted by issuer disclosure, but are not independently verified risk-free/excess cash.|
|2026Q3 issuer announcement discovered; provider numeric data not yet verified against official-host rawPDF, do not hide negative YTD CFO or call annual cash bridge current.|
|No CODEX, training, probability, account or qualification change made.|
|BASELINE_RESEARCH only:2025 financial/bridge basis, actual current acquisition and discount-rate assumptions. Current pricing is blocked pending2026Q3 reconciliation, commitments and post-quarter share cancellation verification; Q3 cash/investment assets differ materially from2025.|
|Operating cash reserve and collectible related deposits are assumptions; no independent credit appraisal. Noncash financial investment book residual0 is not an appraised economic recovery value.|
|Core margin excludes gains and impairments by explicit definition;2024-margin benchmark and22% stress are assumptions, not forecasts or full-cycle normalization.|
|Capital employed includes explicitly retained operating cash and gross repurchase liability; stable capital-turnover/zero-growth model only. No price-implied reverse analysis, probabilities or expected-return assertion.|
|WACC uses CNY government-yield riskfree proxy without sovereign-spread subtraction, China totalERP includes country risk once, Jan2026 global industry asset-beta proxy relevered by approximate2025 marketE and book interestD. These are justified proxies with model risk, not a measured issuer beta/current market WACC; simplified common beta scaling of totalERP is explicit.|
|Reported2025 material commitment disclosure is none; recognized capital-project payables are deducted once. This does not imply no future replacement spending or optional project plans. Quarter operating-expense cash reserve is an assumption including noncash expenses.|
